{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"qwen3.5-plus","id":"msg_b0343d12-a62e-4ccc-bdd4-6bb5771bbce4","role":"assistant","type":"message","content":[{"type":"thinking","signature":"","thinking":"I see the issue now. The project uses:\n1. `jakarta.validation-api` 3.0.2 (provided scope)\n2. `pm-wicket-utils` 5.0 - which contains `HibernateValidatorProperty` that references `javax.validation.ValidatorFactory`\n3. `hibernate-validator` 6.2.0.Final (test scope) - this is the old version that uses javax.validation\n\nThe problem is that `HibernateValidatorProperty` from `pm-wicket-utils` is using `javax.validation.ValidatorFactory`. Since Jakarta Validation 3.x changed the package namespace from `javax.validation` to `jakarta.validation`, the old library is incompatible.\n\nThere are several ways to fix this:\n1. Use a newer version of `pm-wicket-utils` that supports jakarta.validation (if available)\n2. Add both javax.validation-api (for the pm-wicket-utils library) and jakarta.validation-api\n3. Create a local replacement for the `HibernateValidatorProperty` class\n\nLet me check if there's a newer version of pm-wicket-utils available. Actually, looking at the error message more carefully:\n\n```\n/wicket-crudifier/src/main/java/com/premiumminds/wicket/crudifier/form/elements/ListControlGroups.java:[121,82] cannot access javax.validation.ValidatorFactory\n  class file for javax.validation.ValidatorFactory not found\n```\n\nThe issue is that the code at line 121 uses `HibernateValidatorProperty.validatorFactory` which is of type `javax.validation.ValidatorFactory`. Since we're only providing `jakarta.validation-api` and not `javax.validation-api`, the compiler can't find the class.\n\nThe simplest fix is to add `javax.validation-api` as a dependency (so the pm-wicket-utils library can work) alongside the jakarta.validation-api. But this might not be ideal since it mixes both APIs.\n\n\nActually, I need to reconsider the constraint here. The user specifically wants to bump `jakarta.validation:jakarta.validation-api` from 2.0.2 to 3.0.2, which means the project was already on Jakarta Validation 2.x. The real problem is that `pm-wicket-utils` 5.0 was built against the older javax.validation API and needs to be updated to support Jakarta Validation 3.x. The core issue is a version mismatch. The `pm-wicket-utils` 5.0 library expects `javax.validation`, but the project is using `jakarta.validation-api` 3.0.2. Since Jakarta Validation 3.x renamed the package namespace, these are incompatible.\n\nI could add `javax.validation-api` as a dependency to satisfy the library's requirements, but that creates a mixed environment.
